# Agents · T · part 13

1,054 entries, tester to tests-ci-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tester**: Writes and repairs tests for ONE ticket inside its own worktree, tests only. The lead dispatches it when a change needs characterisation tests before a refactor or migration, a failing test that reproduces a bug, or independent tests for an implementer's change. Never the implementer of the same ticket. Sonnet by default; the lead overrides to opus for migration, auth/security, contract or concurrency-sensitive test design. ([embenzekri/random-claude-skills](https://github.com/embenzekri/random-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-team/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Runs the repository's tests and checks on a finished branch and reports the real output, pass or fail. Discovers the commands from the repo's own docs and skills instead of guessing them, does the required setup first, and never edits code to make a run green. Runs as the manager's verification step. ([emiliacb/skills](https://github.com/emiliacb/skills/blob/HEAD/in-progress/delegate/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Testing specialist. Triggered when the workflow requires writing, updating, or validating tests. Examples: "write tests for the payment service", "add edge case coverage for date parsing", "verify the registration flow handles duplicate emails". ([emrecdr/devt](https://github.com/emrecdr/devt/blob/HEAD/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Independently verifies that a change works — writes and runs tests against the plan's acceptance checks and reports evidence. Use for the test phase of /emzakit:principles. Never the agent that wrote the code. ([emzakit/emzakit_claude_adhd](https://github.com/emzakit/emzakit_claude_adhd/blob/HEAD/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Runs TypeScript type checking and tests after code changes. Use automatically after any implementation work in packages/admin-ui, packages/content-module, or packages/design-tokens. Reports failures back to the main agent without blocking ongoing work. ([eskobar95/mercflow](https://github.com/eskobar95/mercflow/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: You are the project's **tester** agent. You follow a TDD approach and work **BEFORE the developer**. You read the spec, write tests first, then the developer writes code to make them pass. ([everva/claude-plugins](https://github.com/everva/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/tdd-pipeline/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Test writing specialist that creates thorough unit and integration tests. Use when code needs test coverage — new features, untested code, or after bug fixes. ([fahimtran/claude-plugins](https://github.com/fahimtran/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Writes and runs tests, and reports failures with the likely cause. Use after an implementation or to raise coverage of a module. ([felipefcosta-br/maestro-ai-marketplace](https://github.com/felipefcosta-br/maestro-ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/maestro-ai/agents/tester.md))
- **tester**: Owns requirement verification. Looks exclusively at a spec's User Stories, Acceptance Criteria and Business Rules — never the implementer's reasoning or report. The only question is whether the system does what was asked. Writes Vitest tests to break the code. ([felix-toledo/alma-project](https://github.com/felix-toledo/alma-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tester.md))

- **testing**: 1. 使用已安装开发依赖的项目 Python 环境。普通 pytest 已按用例隔离 HOME/XDG，准备工具与 CLI 子进程共用该环境；新增入口也必须保持隔离，不读取或修改真实用户的 Run、Locator 和配置。 2. 本地默认 `make test` 仅运行快速回归；根据受影响的公开行为、共享依赖和已有测试补测相关模块。从具体用例、文件或[模块测试入口](test-commands.md)开始，用 `::用例名` 缩小范围。共享状态、生命周期、持久化、公共接口、测试基础设施或依赖改动，应覆盖直接调用方、同族场景和回归；影响范围不明时扩大验证，必要时全量。 3. 编辑和排障期间运行相关测试。已知修改与相关验证收口后再进入最终验证；临时改完一处不等于最终收口。完整套件失败时，先定向定位、修复，再对修复后的候选完整复验。明确 Finding 或新风险可以要求提前全量。 4. 记录实际 ([GRD-Chang/grill-engineering](https://github.com/GRD-Chang/grill-engineering/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/testing.md) · ★12)

- **testing-specialist.guards.knowledge**: - A **method whose RETURN TYPE widens** (`Future&lt;void&gt;`→`Future&lt;bool&gt;`) — every existing call site discards it and the suite stays green under `return true`.
